norm_label(s: str) -> str:\n    # Normalize therapy label/name for matching across aliases.\n    # - case-insensitive\n    # - treat hyphens/underscores as spaces\n    # - collapse whitespace\n    if s is None:\n        return \"\"\n    s = str(s).strip().lower()\n    s = s.replace(\"-\", \" \").replace(\"_\", \" \")\n    s = \" \".join(s.split())\n    return s\n\n\ndef read_csv(path: str):\n float(t[\"drug_cost_per_1000_mg_usd\"])\n            * active_patients\n            * float(t[\"dose_mg_per_day\"])\n            * days_per_delivery\n            * deliveries_per_year\n            / annual_margin = annual_revenue - annual_drug_cost - annual_supply_cost\n        return {\n            \"annual_drug_cost\": annual_drug_cost,\n            \"annual_supply_cost\": annual_supply_cost,\n for {code}\")\n        bag_size = int(float(t[\"bag_size_ml\"]))\n        if bag_size not in bag_cost_by_size:\n            missing.append(f\"missing bag supply cost for bag_size_ml={bag_size}     r7 = compute_for_model(\n            t,\n            active_patients,\n            payment,\n            bag_cost,\n            days_per_delivery=ASSUMPTIONS[\"days_per_delivery_7_day\"],\n            deliveries_per_year=ASSUMPTIONS[\"deliveries_per_year_7_day\"],\n        )\n        r14 = compute_for_model(\n            t,\n            active_patients,\n            payment,\n            bag_cost,\n            days_per_delivery=ASSUMPTIONS[\"days_per_delivery_14_day\"],\n            deliveries_per_year=ASSUMPTIONS[\"deliveries_per_year_14_day\"],\n        )\n\n        therapies_out.append(\n            {\n                \"therapy_code\": code,\n                \"therapy_name\": t.get(\"therapy_name\"),\n                \"active_patients\": int(active_patients),\n                \"bag_size_ml\": int(bag_size),\n                \"bag_supply_cost_usd\": money(bag_cost),\n                \"payment_per_delivery_per_patient_usd\": money(payment),\n        \"annual_supply_cost_14_day_usd\": money(r14[\"annual_supply_cost\"]),\n                \"annual_revenue_14_day_usd\": money(r14[\"annual_revenue\"]),\n                \"annual_margin_14_day_usd\": money(r14[\"annual_margin\"]),\n                \"annual_margin_difference_14_minus_7_usd\": money(\n                    money(r14[\"annual_margin\"]) - money(r7[\"annual_margin\"])\n                ),\n            }\n        )\n\n    therapies_out.sort(key=lambda x: x[\"therapy_code\"])\n\n    # --- Totals ---\n    total_7 = money(sum(t[\"annual_margin_7_day_usd\"] for t in therapies_out))\n    total_14 = money(sum(t[\"annual_margin_14_day_usd\"] for t in therapies_out))\n    total_diff = money(sum(t[\"annual_margin_difference_14_minus_7_usd\"] for t in therapies_out))\n    abs_total_diff = money(abs(total_diff))\n\n    if abs_total_diff < ASSUMPTIONS[\"switch_threshold_usd\"]:\n        decision_slug = \"move_to_14_day\"\n        justification = (\n            f\"Absolute total margin difference ${fmt_usd(abs_total_diff)} is below the $\"\n            f\"{fmt_usd(ASSUMPTIONS['switch_threshold_usd'])} threshold; 14-day batching is financially comparable.\"\n        )\n    else:\n        decision_slug = \"keep_7_day\"\n        justification = (\n            f\"Absolute total margin difference ${fmt_usd(abs_total_diff)} meets/exceeds the $\"\n            f\"{fmt_usd(ASSUMPTIONS['switch_threshold_usd'])} threshold; keep 7-day batching.\"\n        )\n\n    out = {\n        \"assumptions\": ASSUMPTIONS,\n        \"therapies\": therapies_out,\n        \"totals\": {\n            \"total_annual_margin_7_day_usd\": total_7,\n            \"total_annual_margin_14_day_usd\": total_14,\n            \"total_annual_margin_difference_14_minus_7_usd\": total_diff,\n            \"absolute_total_margin_difference_usd\": abs_total_diff,\n        },\n        \"decision\": {\n            \"decision\": decision_slug,\n            \"justification\": justification,\n        },\n    }\n\n    with open(OUT_JSON_PATH, \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(out, f, indent=2)\n        f.write(\"\\n\")\n\n    # 4–8 non-empty lines\n    lines = [\n        f\"Total annual margin (7-day): {fmt_usd(total_7)} USD\",\n        f\"Total annual margin (14-day): {fmt_usd(total_14)} USD\",\n        f\"Absolute difference: {fmt_usd(abs_total_diff)} USD\",\n        f\"Decision: {decision_slug}\",\n    ]\n\n    with open(OUT_MD_PATH, \"w\") as f:\n float(t[\"drug_cost_per_1000_mg_usd\"])\n            * active_patients\n            * float(t[\"dose_mg_per_day\"])\n            * days_per_delivery\n            * deliveries_per_year\n            / 1000.0\n        )\n        annual_supply_cost = bag_cost * active_patients * deliveries_per_year\n        annual_revenue = payment_per_delivery * active_patients * deliveries_per_year\n        annual_margin = annual_revenue - annual_drug_cost - annual_supply_cost\n        return {\n            \"annual_drug_cost\": annual_drug_cost,\n            \"annual_supply_cost\": annual_supply_cost,\n               \"bag_size_ml\": int(bag_size),\n                \"bag_supply_cost_usd\": money(bag_cost),\n                \"payment_per_delivery_per_patient_usd\": money(payment),\n  decision_slug = \"move_to_14_day\"\n        justification = (\n            f\"Absolute total margin difference ${fmt_usd(abs_total_diff)} is below the $\"\n            f\"{fmt_usd(ASSUMPTIONS['switch_threshold_usd'])} threshold; 14-day batching is financially comparable.\"\n        )\n    else:\n        decision_slug = \"keep_7_day\"\n        justification = (\n            f\"Absolute total margin difference ${fmt_usd(abs_total_diff)} meets/exceeds the $\"\n            f\"{fmt_usd(ASSUMPTIONS['switch_threshold_usd'])} threshold; keep 7-day batching.\"\n
